       res.end('{\"input_tokens\":42}');\n      });\n    });\n    fake.listen(0, '127.0.0.1', () => {\n      const porta = fake.address().port;\n      const cfg = { byok: { enabled: true, mode: 'always', baseUrl: 'http://127.0.0.1:' + porta,\n        headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer TOKEN-DO-ENDPOINT' } } };\n      const cliente = { 'authorization': 'Bearer sk-ant-oat-SEGREDO', 'x-api-key': 'sk-ant-CHAVE',\n        'anthropic-version': '2023-06-01' };\n      const sink = new PassThrough(); sink.writeHead = () => {}; sink.headersSent = false;\n      rs.passthrough(JSON.stringify({ model: 'claude-haiku-4-5', messages: [] }),\n        cliente, sink, '/v1/messages/count_tokens', cfg);\n      setTimeout(() => {\n        fake.close();\n        assert(got, 'count_tokens NAO chegou ao endpoint do usuario — foi para a Anthropic');\n        const h = JSON.stringify(got.headers);\n        assert(got.path === '/v1/messages/count_tokens', 'path preservado, veio ' + got.path);\n        assert(got.headers.authorization === 'Bearer TOKEN-DO-ENDPOINT', 'header configurado deveria valer: ' + h);\n        assert(!/sk-ant-oat/.test(h), 'VAZAMENTO: token da assinatura no count_tokens');\n        assert(!/sk-ant-CHAVE/.test(h), 'VAZAMENTO: x-api-key da assinatura no count_tokens');\n        process.exit(0);\n      }, 900);\n    });\n  `;\n  const body = `const assert=(c,m)=>{if(!c){console.error('FAIL: '+m);process.exit(1)}};`\n    + `try{${script}}catch(e){console.error(e&&e.stack||e);process.exit(1)}`;\n  try {\n    require('child_process').execFileSync(process.execPath, ['-e', body], {\n      env: { ...process.env, CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA: fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'ccb-ct-')) },\n      stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'], timeout: 30000,\n    });\n  } catch (e) {\n    throw new Error((e.stderr && e.stderr.toString().trim()) || e.message);\n  }\n});\n\ntest('passthrough SEM byok: continua na Anthropic com a credencial (nao regride)', () => {\n  // O caminho normal nao pode mudar: sem BYOK, count_tokens vai para a Anthropic\n  // e a credencial SEGUE — o destino e o dono dela.\n  const up = byok.resolveUpstream({}, { onLimit: false });\n  assertEq(up.isByok, false);\n  assertEq(up.host, 'api.anthropic.com');\n  const h = byok.buildHeaders({ 'authorization': 'Bearer oat', 'x-api-key': 'k' }, up);\n  assertEq(h.authorization, 'Bearer oat');\n  assertEq(h['x-api-key'], 'k');\n});\n\ntest('byok mode=on-limit: count_tokens NAO desvia (o Claude ainda e quem atende)', () => {\n  // Em on-limit o fluxo normal e do Claude — desviar a contagem criaria a MESMA\n  // divergencia, so que ao contrario.\n  const cfg = { byok: { enabled: true, mode: 'on-limit', baseUrl: 'https://ex.ts.net', headers: {} } };\n  assertEq(byok.resolveUpstream(cfg, { onLimit: false }).isByok, false,\n    'contagem e geracao precisam concordar: ambas no Claude enquanto ele atende');\n});\n\n\ntest('byok.resolveUpstream: respeita o DEFAULT injetado (preserva o override de env dos testes)', () => {\n  // ROUTER_UPSTREAM_HOST/PORT/PROTOCOL existem p/ apontar o proxy a um servidor\n  // fake em teste. Se o destino padrao ficar hardcoded aqui, esse mecanismo\n  // morre silenciosamente — e as constantes do server viram codigo morto.\n  const fallback = { host: '127.0.0.1', port: 9999, protocol: 'http:' };\n  const u = byok.resolveUpstream({}, { onLimit: false }, fallback);\n  assertEq(u.isByok, false);\n  assertEq(u.host, '127.0.0.1');\n  assertEq(u.port, 9999);\n  assertEq(u.protocol, 'http:');\n});\n\ntest('byok.resolveUpstream: sem fallback injetado, o default continua a Anthropic', () => {\n  const u = byok.resolveUpstream({}, { onLimit: false });\n  assertEq(u.host, 'api.anthropic.com');\n  assertEq(u.port, 443);\n  assertEq(u.protocol, 'https:');\n});\n\ntest('byok.resolveUpstream: o BYOK VENCE o fallback injetado (destino do usuario manda)', () => {\n  const cfg = { byok: { enabled: true, mode: 'always', baseUrl: 'https://ex.ts.net', headers: {} } };\n  const u = byok.resolveUpstream(cfg, { onLimit: false }, { host: '127.0.0.1', port: 9999, protocol: 'http:' });\n  assertEq(u.host, 'ex.ts.net');\n  assertEq(u.isByok, true);\n});\n\ntest('byok: o CATALOGO de modelos tambem segue o destino (mesma classe do count_tokens)', () => {\n  // Segundo furo da mesma familia: `maybeWarmCatalog` apontava fixo para a\n  // Anthropic e mandava a credencial da assinatura. Com BYOK ligado, o catalogo\n  // listaria os modelos de um provedor enquanto a geracao acontece em outro — e\n  // o contrato do endpoint diz que ele expoe /v1/models com os MESMOS headers.\n  // Menos grave que a janela (nao oscila nada), mas e a mesma inconsistencia.\n  const cfg = { byok: { enabled: true, mode: 'always', baseUrl: 'http://10.0.0.5:4143', headers: { Authorization: 'Bearer T' } } };\n  const alvo = byok.resolveUpstream(cfg, { onLimit: false });\n  assertEq(alvo.isByok, true);\n  assertEq(alvo.host, '10.0.0.5');\n  assertEq(alvo.port, 4143);\n  // E os headers do catalogo nao podem levar a credencial da assinatura.\n  const h = byok.buildHeaders({ 'x-api-key': 'sk-ant-CHAVE', 'authorization': 'Bearer sk-ant-oat' }, alvo);\n  assertEq(h['x-api-key'], undefined);\n  assertEq(h.authorization, undefined);\n  assertEq(h.Authorization, 'Bearer T');\n});\n\ntest('cooldownActive: false por padrao, true durante a janela armada, false apos reset', () => {\n  const rs = require('../servers/model-router/index.js');\n  rs.__testHooks.reset();\n  try {\n    assertEq(rs.cooldownActive({}), false, 'sem cooldown armado, disjuntor tem que estar OFF');\n    rs.__testHooks.setCooldownUntil(Date.now() + 60000);\n    assertEq(rs.cooldownActive({}), true, 'janela armada no futuro, disjuntor tem que estar ON');\n    rs.__testHooks.setCooldownUntil(Date.now() - 1000);\n    assertEq(rs.cooldownActive({}), false, 'janela ja expirada, disjuntor tem que estar OFF');\n    // enabled:false desarma mesmo com _cooldownUntil no futuro (a config manda).\n    rs.__testHooks.setCooldownUntil(Date.now() + 60000);\n    assertEq(rs.cooldownActive({ fallback: { cooldown: { enabled: false } } }), false,\n      'cooldown.enabled=false tem que desarmar mesmo com janela no futuro');\n  } finally {\n    rs.__testHooks.reset();\n  }\n});\n\ntest('sanitizeUpstreamHeaders: remove transfer-encoding/content-length/connection do upstream (bug do streaming pulando com BYOK)', () => {\n  // O parser HTTP do Node ja DESENQUADRA o transfer-encoding:chunked do upRes\n  // antes de emitir 'data' (o corpo que chega em upRes.pipe(res) esta SEM o\n  // framing). Repassar o header transfer-encoding do upstream verbatim faz o\n  // CLIENTE esperar bytes com aquele framing, que nunca chegam — o parser dele\n  // perde sincronia (visto como \"o streaming pula e volta\" a cada request nova,\n  // so com o gateway BYOK, cujo chunking/compressao diverge da Anthropic direta).\n  // content-length/connection tem o mesmo risco: sao da conexao ANTERIOR.\n  const rs = require('../servers/model-router/index.js');\n  const sujo = {\n    'transfer-encoding': 'chunked',\n    'content-length': '123',\n    'connection': 'keep-alive',\n    'keep-alive': 'timeout=5',\n    'content-type': 'text/event-stream; charset=utf-8',\n    'x-request-id': 'abc123',\n  };\n  const limpo = rs.sanitizeUpstreamHeaders(sujo);\n  assertEq(limpo['transfer-encoding'], undefined, 'transfer-encoding tem que ser removido');\n  assertEq(limpo['content-length'], undefined, 'content-length tem que ser removido');\n  assertEq(limpo['connection'], undefined, 'connection tem que ser removido');\n  assertEq(limpo['keep-alive'], undefined, 'keep-alive tem que ser removido');\n  assertEq(limpo['content-type'], 'text/event-stream; charset=utf-8', 'content-type tem que sobreviver');\n  assertEq(limpo['x-request-id'], 'abc123', 'headers inofensivos tem que sobreviver');\n  // Case-insensitive e nao muta o objeto original.\n  assertEq(sujo['transfer-encoding'], 'chunked', 'input original nao pode ser mutado');\n  const vazio = rs.sanitizeUpstreamHeaders(null);\n  assertEq(Object.keys(vazio).length, 0, 'headers ausentes nao pode lancar, devolve objeto vazio');\n});\n\ntest('byok on-limit BUG DE CAMPO 2: count_tokens/catalogo ficavam presos na Anthropic durante o cooldown', () => {\n  // Com byok.mode=on-limit, forwardRequest desvia a GERACAO pro endpoint do\n  // usuario assim que o disjuntor arma (cooldown ativo, ver armCooldown/\n  // handleLimitExceeded). Mas passthrough()/maybeWarmCatalog() resolviam com\n  // `onLimit: false` HARDCODED — ou seja, na MESMA janela em que a geracao ja\n  // esta indo pro BYOK, a contagem de tokens e o catalogo continuavam presos\n  // na Anthropic. Mesma classe de bug do v2.21.3 (destinos divergentes),\n  // reaberta so nessa janela de fallback ativo.\n  const script = `\n    const http = require('http');\n    const { PassThrough } = require('stream');\n    const rs = require(${JSON.stringify(path.join(ROOT, 'servers', 'model-router', 'index.js'))});\n    let got = null;\n    const fake = http.createServer((req, res) => {\n      let body = '';\n      req.on('data', (c) => { body += c; });\n      req.on('end', () => {\n        got = { headers: req.headers, path: req.url, body };\n        res.writeHead(200, { 'content-type': 'application/json' });\n        res.end('{\"input_tokens\":7}');\n      });\n    });\n    fake.listen(0, '127.0.0.1', () => {\n      const porta = fake.address().port;\n      const cfg = { byok: { enabled: true, mode: 'on-limit', baseUrl: 'http://127.0.0.1:' + porta,\n        headers: { 'x-fake-key': 'valor-fake-de-teste' } } };\n      const cliente = { 'x-fake-key': 'valor-nao-deveria-ir', 'anthropic-version': '2023-06-01' };\n      // Disjuntor ARMADO: e o que forwardRequest ja teria feito ao desviar a\n      // geracao pro plano B. Sem o fix, passthrough ignora isso (onLimit fixo\n      // em false) e a request cai na Anthropic real — 502/timeout no teste,\n      // nunca no fake local.\n      rs.__testHooks.setCooldownUntil(Date.now() + 60000);\n      const sink = new PassThrough(); sink.writeHead = () => {}; sink.headersSent = false;\n      rs.passthrough(JSON.stringify({ model: 'claude-haiku-4-5', messages: [] }),\n        cliente, sink, '/v1/messages/count_tokens', cfg);\n      setTimeout(() => {\n        fake.close();\n        assert(got, 'count_tokens NAO chegou ao endpoint do usuario durante o cooldown — ficou preso na Anthropic');\n        assertEq(got.path, '/v1/messages/count_tokens');\n        assertEq(got.headers['x-fake-key'], 'valor-fake-de-teste', 'header configurado deveria valer');\n        process.exit(0);\n      }, 900);\n    });\n    function assertEq(a, b, m) { if (a !== b) { console.error('FAIL: ' + (m || '') + ' esperado=' + b + ' obtido=' + a); process.exit(1); } }\n  `;\n  const bodyScript = `const assert=(c,m)=>{if(!c){console.error('FAIL: '+m);process.exit(1)}};`\n    + `try{${script}}catch(e){console.error(e&&e.stack||e);process.exit(1)}`;\n  try {\n    require('child_process').execFileSync(process.execPath, ['-e', bodyScript], {\n      env: { ...process.env, CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA: fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'ccb-ct-cd-')) },\n      stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'], timeout: 30000,\n    });\n  } catch (e) {\n    throw new Error((e.stderr && e.stderr.toString().trim()) || e.message);\n  }\n});\n\ntest('gateway alternativo NAO arma o disjuntor da Anthropic (achado da auditoria 2026-09-11)', () => {\n  // config.upstream (gateway corporativo tipo LiteLLM) resolve isByok:false,\n  // isCustomEndpoint:true — o MESMO destino \"nao-Anthropic\" que ja motivou o fix\n  // de timeout desta sessao. forwardRequest usava so `!isByok` pra decidir se\n  // arma/consulta o cooldown global: um 429 do GATEWAY (que pode ser so o\n  // rate-limit proprio dele) armava o mesmo `_cooldownUntil` que devia refletir\n  // so a janela da assinatura Anthropic real, desviando geracao+catalogo por um\n  // limite que nada tem a ver com a Anthropic. Prova: 429 com retry-after (arma\n  // na hora, ver armCooldown/computeCooldownUntil) atraves do gateway NAO pode\n  // deixar rastro em _cooldownUntil.\n  const script = `\n    const http = require('http');\n    const { PassThrough } = require('stream');\n    const rs = require(${JSON.stringify(path.join(ROOT, 'servers', 'model-router', 'index.js'))});\n    const fake = http.createServer((req, res) => {\n      req.on('data', () => {});\n      req.on('end', () => {\n        res.writeHead(429, { 'content-type': 'application/json', 'retry-after': '60' });\n        res.end('{\"error\":{\"message\":\"gateway rate limit\"}}');\n      });\n    });\n    fake.listen(0, '127.0.0.1', () => {\n      const porta = fake.address().port;\n      const cfg = { upstream: { enabled: true, baseUrl: 'http://127.0.0.1:' + porta } };\n      const cliente = { 'x-api-key': 'sk-ant-CHAVE-cliente', 'anthropic-version': '2023-06-01' };\n      const sink = new PassThrough(); sink.writeHead = () => {}; sink.headersSent = false;\n      rs.__testHooks.reset();\n      rs.forwardRequest({ model: 'claude-haiku-4-5', messages: [] }, cliente, sink, cfg,\n        { origTier: 'haiku', finalTier: 'haiku', path: '/v1/messages' });\n      setTimeout(() => {\n        fake.close();\n        const estado = rs.__testHooks.getState();\n        assertEq(estado.until, 0, 'um 429 do GATEWAY nao pode armar o cooldown da Anthropic — armou ate ' + estado.until);\n        process.exit(0);\n      }, 900);\n    });\n    function assertEq(a, b, m) { if (a !== b) { console.error('FAIL: ' + (m || '') + ' esperado=' + b + ' obtido=' + a); process.exit(1); } }\n  `;\n  const bodyScript = `const assert=(c,m)=>{if(!c){console.error('FAIL: '+m);process.exit(1)}};`\n    + `try{${script}}catch(e){console.error(e&&e.stack||e);process.exit(1)}`;\n  try {\n    require('child_process').execFileSync(process.execPath, ['-e', bodyScript], {\n      env: { ...process.env, CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA: fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'ccb-gw-cd-')) },\n      stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'], timeout: 30000,\n    });\n  } catch (e) {\n    throw new Error((e.stderr && e.stderr.toString().trim()) || e.message);\n  }\n});\n\ntest('byok.classifyResponse: 200 → ok', () => {\n  assertEq(byok.classifyResponse(200, '').ok, true);\n});\n\ntest('byok.classifyResponse: 401/404/502 → FAIL LOUD (repetir nao resolve)', () => {\n  for (const s of [401, 404, 502]) {\n    const r = byok.classifyResponse(s, '');\n    assertEq(r.ok, false, 'status ' + s);\n    assertEq(r.failLoud, true, 'status ' + s + ' precisa gritar');\n    assertEq(r.retryable, false, 'status ' + s + ' nao adianta repetir');\n  }\n  assertEq(/credencial|dashboard/i.test(byok.classifyResponse(401, '').reason), true,\n    '401 diz que a credencial do dashboard esta errada');\n});\n\ntest('byok.classifyResponse: 429 → RETENTAVEL (teto por credencial, nao erro de config)', () => {\n  const r = byok.classifyResponse(429, '');\n  assertEq(r.retryable, true);\n  assertEq(r.failLoud, false);\n});\n\ntest('byok.classifyResponse: corpo com \"model is not supported\" → fail loud mesmo em 200', () => {\n  const r = byok.classifyResponse(200, '{\"error\":{\"message\":\"model is not supported\"}}');\n  assertEq(r.ok, false);\n  assertEq(r.failLoud, true);\n});\n\ntest('byok: o modelo e repassado VERBATIM (o endpoint normaliza; mapear seria errar)', () => {\n  // Contrato verificado: claude-haiku-4-5 → servido como claude-haiku-4.5;\n  // inexistente → erro explicito. Normalizar aqui so criaria divergencia.\n  assertEq(typeof byok.mapModel, 'undefined', 'nao pode existir mapeamento de nome');\n});\n\ntest('resolveMode: byok always liga o proxy quando nada mais esta ligado', () => {\n  const rm = require('./lib/router-mode.js');\n  assertEq(rm.resolveMode({ byok: { enabled: true, mode: 'always' } }), 'byok-direct');\n  assertEq(rm.resolveMode({ byok: { enabled: true, mode: 'on-limit' } }), 'fallback-only',\n    'on-limit precisa da mesma postura do fallback: passthrough + intervir no 429');\n  assertEq(rm.resolveMode({ byok: { enabled: false, mode: 'always' } }), 'off');\n});\n\ntest('resolveMode: byok NAO rouba a precedencia de sticky (destino e ortogonal a rota)', () => {\n  const rm = require('./lib/router-mode.js');\n  assertEq(rm.resolveMode({ sticky: { enabled: true }, byok: { enabled: true, mode: 'always' } }), 'sticky-tier');\n});\n\ntest('modeMeta: byok-direct tem rotulo e cor proprios (nao cai no fail-safe off)', () => {\n  const rm = require('./lib/router-mode.js');\n  const m = rm.modeMeta('byok-direct');\n  assertEq(m.i18n !== rm.modeMeta('off').i18n, true, 'precisa de rotulo proprio');\n});\n\ntest('byok INTEGRACAO: o token da assinatura NAO chega ao endpoint (prova ponta a ponta)', () => {\n  // O teste mais importante deste modulo: sobe um endpoint FALSO, manda uma\n  // request pelo forwardRequest com o header da assinatura, e inspeciona o que\n  // o endpoint REALMENTE recebeu. Se o `authorization` da assinatura aparecer\n  // ali, a credencial do usuario vazou p/ um terceiro.\n  //\n  // Roda em SUBPROCESSO (como runBrainScenario): o harness executa os testes em\n  // concorrencia, e um servidor HTTP + espera aqui dentro disputava o event loop\n  // e fazia OUTROS testes de rede estourarem timeout. Isolado, e deterministico.\n  const script = `\n    const http = require('http');\n    const { PassThrough } = require('stream');\n    const rs = require(${JSON.stringify(path.join(ROOT, 'servers', 'model-router', 'index.js'))});\n    let got = null;\n    const fake = http.createServer((req, res) => {\n      let body = '';\n      req.on('data', (c) => { body += c; });\n      req.on('end', () => {\n        got = { headers: req.headers, path: req.url, body };\n        res.writeHead(200, { 'content-type': 'application/json' });\n        res.end('{\"ok\":true}');\n      });\n    });\n    fake.listen(0, '127.0.0.1', () => {\n      const porta = fake.address().port;\n      const cfg = { byok: { enabled: true, mode: 'always', baseUrl: 'http://127.0.0.1:' + porta,\n        headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer TOKEN-DO-ENDPOINT', 'X-Tenant-Id': 'acme' } } };\n      const cliente = { 'authorization': 'Bearer sk-ant-oat-SEGREDO', 'x-api-key': 'sk-ant-CHAVE',\n        'anthropic-version': '2023-06-01' };\n      const sink = new PassThrough(); sink.writeHead = () => {}; sink.headersSent = false;\n      rs.forwardRequest({ model: 'claude-haiku-4-5', messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'oi' }] },\n        cliente, sink, cfg, { origTier: 'haiku', finalTier: 'haiku', path: '/v1/messages' });\n      setTimeout(() => {\n        fake.close();\n        const h = JSON.stringify(got && got.headers || {});\n        assert(got, 'o endpoint precisa ter recebido a request');\n        assert(got.headers.authorization === 'Bearer TOKEN-DO-ENDPOINT', 'header configurado deveria valer, veio: ' + h);\n        assert(!/sk-ant-oat/.test(h), 'VAZAMENTO: token da assinatura chegou ao endpoint');\n        assert(!/sk-ant-CHAVE/.test(h), 'VAZAMENTO: x-api-key da assinatura chegou ao endpoint');\n        assert(got.headers['x-tenant-id'] === 'acme', 'headers extras do mapa generico devem chegar');\n        assert(got.path === '/v1/messages', 'path deve ser /v1/messages, veio ' + got.path);\n        assert(/claude-haiku-4-5/.test(got.body), 'o model vai VERBATIM, sem mapeamento');\n        process.exit(0);\n      }, 900);\n    });\n  `;\n  const body = `const assert=(c,m)=>{if(!c){console.error('FAIL: '+m);process.exit(1)}};`\n    + `try{${script}}catch(e){console.error(e&&e.stack||e);process.exit(1)}`;\n  try {\n    require('child_process').execFileSync(process.execPath, ['-e', body], {\n      env: { ...process.env, CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA: fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'ccb-byok-')) },\n      stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'], timeout: 30000,\n    });\n  } catch (e) {\n    throw new Error((e.stderr && e.stderr.toString().trim()) || e.message);\n  }\n});\n\n// ─── TTL OBSERVADO: parar de adivinhar a janela do cache ─────────────────────\n// O `accumulateUsage` ja LE `ephemeral_1h_input_tokens` e `ephemeral_5m_input_tokens`\n// (a propria resposta da API declara qual janela foi CONTRATADA no write), mas isso\n// so refinava o TTL interno e era jogado fora. Extraido do binario do Claude Code:\n//   let P = tFe(e.querySource) ? \"1h\" : void 0;\n//   function tFe(e){ if(env.FORCE_PROMPT_CACHING_5M) return false;\n//                    if(env.ENABLE_PROMPT_CACHING_1H) return true;\n//                    if(!ii() || Kie().isUsingOverage) return false;\n//                    let t=lvi(); if(t===null) t=Ke(\"tengu_prompt_cache_1h_config\",{allowlist:...\n// Ou seja: a janela pode ser 1h por FEATURE-FLAG REMOTO da Anthropic (allowlist por\n// querySource) ou por overage — o usuario NAO controla e nao tem como saber por\n// inspecao. Persistir o que a resposta declara transforma isso de palpite em medida.\n\ntest('metricsTtl: acumula o write por JANELA CONTRATADA (5m vs 1h)', () => {\n  routerServer.resetMetrics();\n  const m0 = routerServer.metricsSnapshot();\n  assertEq(m0.ttl.write5m, 0, 'baseline existe desde o boot');\n  assertEq(m0.ttl.write1h, 0);\n  assertEq(m0.ttl.observed, null, 'sem write observado, NAO afirma janela nenhuma');\n\n  routerServer.metricsTtl({ cacheTtl5m: 12000, cacheTtl1h: 0 });\n  routerServer.metricsTtl({ cacheTtl5m: 8000, cacheTtl1h: 0 });\n  const m = routerServer.metricsSnapshot();\n  assertEq(m.ttl.write5m, 20000);\n  assertEq(m.ttl.write1h, 0);\n  assertEq(m.ttl.observed, '5m', 'o dado MEDIDO responde qual janela o cliente contratou');\n});\n\ntest('metricsTtl: janela de 1h e detectada (o flag remoto da Anthropic pode liga-la)', () => {\n  routerServer.resetMetrics();\n  routerServer.metricsTtl({ cacheTtl5m: 0, cacheTtl1h: 30000 });\n  const m = routerServer.metricsSnapshot();\n  assertEq(m.ttl.write1h, 30000);\n  assertEq(m.ttl.observed, '1h');\n  assertEq(m.ttl.observedMs, 3600000, 'a janela em ms, p/ o detector usar o valor REAL');\n});\n\ntest('metricsTtl: MISTO (mixing TTLs e permitido pela API) → reporta os dois', () => {\n  routerServer.resetMetrics();\n  routerServer.metricsTtl({ cacheTtl5m: 4000, cacheTtl1h: 16000 });\n  const m = routerServer.metricsSnapshot();\n  assertEq(m.ttl.observed, 'mixed', 'nao escolhe um vencedor arbitrario quando ha os dois');\n  assertEq(m.ttl.observedMs, 3600000, 'p/ decisao conservadora, vale a janela MAIS LONGA');\n  assertEq(m.ttl.pct1h, 80, '16000 de 20000 dos writes sao 1h');\n});\n\ntest('metricsTtl: write sem detalhe de janela conta em unknown (nao inventa 5m)', () => {\n  routerServer.resetMetrics();\n  routerServer.metricsTtl({ cacheCreate: 9000, cacheTtl5m: 0, cacheTtl1h: 0 });\n  const m = routerServer.metricsSnapshot();\n  assertEq(m.ttl.writeUnknown, 9000, 'houve write mas a janela nao veio no usage');\n  assertEq(m.ttl.observed, null, 'sem prova, sem afirmacao');\n});\n\ntest('metricsTtl: chunk sem write nenhum e ignorado (nao suja a serie)', () => {\n  routerServer.resetMetrics();\n  routerServer.metricsTtl({ cacheRead: 90000 });\n  routerServer.metricsTtl(null);\n  const m = routerServer.metricsSnapshot();\n  assertEq(m.ttl.write5m + m.ttl.write1h + m.ttl.writeUnknown, 0);\n  assertEq(m.ttl.observed, null);\n});\n\ntest('deriveTtlVerdict: sem writes → tudo null (sem prova, sem afirmacao)', () => {\n  assertEq(cacheCycle.deriveTtlVerdict(null).observed, null);\n  assertEq(cacheCycle.deriveTtlVerdict({}).observedMs, null);\n  assertEq(cacheCycle.deriveTtlVerdict({ write5m: 0, write1h: 0 }).pct1h, null);\n});\n\ntest('deriveTtlVerdict: só 1h → observed \"1h\", observedMs 3600000, pct1h 100', () => {\n  const v = cacheCycle.deriveTtlVerdict({ write5m: 0, write1h: 5000 });\n  assertEq(v.observed, '1h');\n  assertEq(v.observedMs, 3600000);\n  assertEq(v.pct1h, 100);\n});\n\ntest('deriveTtlVerdict: só 5m → observed \"5m\", observedMs 300000, pct1h 0', () => {\n  const v = cacheCycle.deriveTtlVerdict({ write5m: 5000, write1h: 0 });\n  assertEq(v.observed, '5m');\n  assertEq(v.observedMs, 300000);\n  assertEq(v.pct1h, 0);\n});\n\ntest('deriveTtlVerdict: mistura de 5m e 1h → \"mixed\", observedMs na janela MAIS LONGA (conservador)', () => {\n  const v = cacheCycle.deriveTtlVerdict({ write5m: 4000, write1h: 16000 });\n  assertEq(v.observed, 'mixed');\n  assertEq(v.observedMs, 3600000, 'subestimar classificaria fronteira fria com cache ainda vivo');\n  assertEq(v.pct1h, 80);\n});\n\ntest('ttlWindowMs: a janela MEDIDA vence o palpite do config (fim da adivinhacao)', () => {\n  // Sem medicao → cai no default configuravel (comportamento atual, preservado).\n  assertEq(cacheCycle.ttlWindowMs(null, {}), 300000);\n  assertEq(cacheCycle.ttlWindowMs(null, { sticky: { coldBoundaryMs: 900000 } }), 900000);\n  // Com medicao → o dado real manda, mesmo contra o config.\n  assertEq(cacheCycle.ttlWindowMs({ observedMs: 3600000 }, { sticky: { coldBoundaryMs: 900000 } }), 3600000,\n    'se a API DISSE 1h, usar 5min do config classificaria fronteira fria em cache vivo');\n  assertEq(cacheCycle.ttlWindowMs({ observedMs: 300000 }, {}), 300000);\n});\n\n\n// (1) DENOMINADOR ERRADO: prize.inputTokens era `in + cacheCreate` (o que foi\n//     RECONSTRUIDO), mas clearableTokens mede o corpo INTEIRO. Numa fronteira que\n//     ainda leu cache, o denominador colapsa e a conta estoura: o campo real\n//     mostrou gap-expired com 41770 limpaveis / 7030 input = 594%, fisicamente\n//     impossivel (o limpavel e SUBCONJUNTO do prompt). Sao duas grandezas\n//     diferentes e precisam de dois campos.\n// (2) CALIBRACAO ENVIESADA: estimateTotalChars nao contava o array `tools` — e o\n//     Claude Code manda dezenas de schemas de ferramenta em toda request. O ratio\n//     medido veio 1.674 chars/token contra ~3.5-4 esperado: chars subcontado ~2.2x.\n\ntest('estimateTotalChars: conta o array TOOLS (schemas sao parte do prompt cobrado)', () => {\n  const semTools = { system: 's'.repeat(1000), messages: [], tools: [] };\n  const comTools = {\n    system: 's'.repeat(1000),\n    messages: [],\n    tools: [\n      { name: 'bash', description: 'd'.repeat(3000), input_schema: { type: 'object', properties: { command: { type: 'string', description: 'x'.repeat(2000) } } } },\n      { name: 'read', description: 'd'.repeat(3000), input_schema: { type: 'object' } },\n    ],\n  };\n  const base = cacheCycle.estimateTotalChars(semTools);\n  const total = cacheCycle.estimateTotalChars(comTools);\n  assertEq(total > base + 7000, true, 'os schemas precisam entrar; sem eles o fator sai ~2x errado. got ' + total);\n});\n\ntest('estimateTotalChars: body sem tools continua funcionando (nao regride)', () => {\n  assertEq(cacheCycle.estimateTotalChars({ system: 'abc', messages: [] }), 3);\n  assertEq(cacheCycle.estimateTotalChars({ messages: [], tools: null }), 0);\n});\n\ntest('observeCacheCycle: separa RECONSTRUIDO de PROMPT INTEIRO (denominadores distintos)', () => {\n  const states = new Map();\n  const body = { messages: [{ role: 'user', content: [\n    { type: 'tool_result', tool_use_id: 'a', content: 'q'.repeat(40000) },\n  ] }] };\n  // fronteira que AINDA leu cache: in=2000, cacheRead=50000, cacheCreate=8000\n  const r = routerServer.observeCacheCycle('k', { in: 2000, cacheRead: 50000, cacheCreate: 8000 }, 1000, {}, { states, body });\n  assertEq(r.boundary.cold, true);\n  assertEq(r.prize.rebuiltTokens, 10000, 'reconstruido = in + cacheCreate (o CUSTO pago)');\n  assertEq(r.prize.promptTokens, 60000, 'prompt inteiro = in + cacheRead + cacheCreate (o DENOMINADOR)');\n  assertEq(r.prize.clearableTokens <= r.prize.promptTokens, true,\n    'o limpavel e SUBCONJUNTO do prompt — nunca pode passar de 100%');\n});\n\ntest('metricsCacheCycle: acumula prompt e reconstruido em campos SEPARADOS', () => {\n  routerServer.resetMetrics();\n  const m0 = routerServer.metricsSnapshot();\n  assertEq(m0.cacheCycle.coldBoundaryPromptTokens, 0, 'baseline do denominador correto existe');\n  routerServer.metricsCacheCycle({\n    state: { lastCacheState: 'miss' }, boundary: { cold: true, reason: 'gap-expired' },\n    prize: { rebuiltTokens: 10000, promptTokens: 140000, clearableTokens: 68000 },\n  });\n  const m = routerServer.metricsSnapshot();\n  assertEq(m.cacheCycle.coldBoundaryInputTokens, 10000, 'reconstruido');\n  assertEq(m.cacheCycle.coldBoundaryPromptTokens, 140000, 'prompt inteiro');\n  assertEq(m.cacheCycle.byReason['gap-expired'].promptTokens, 140000);\n  assertEq(m.cacheCycle.byReason['gap-expired'].clearableTokens, 68000);\n  routerServer.resetMetrics();\n});\n\ntest('metricsSnapshot: clearablePct sai do PROMPT e nunca passa de 100', () => {\n  routerServer.resetMetrics();\n  routerServer.metricsCacheCycle({\n    state: { lastCacheState: 'miss' }, boundary: { cold: true, reason: 'gap-expired' },\n    prize: { rebuiltTokens: 7030, promptTokens: 200000, clearableTokens: 41770 },\n  });\n  const m = routerServer.metricsSnapshot();\n  assertEq(m.cacheCycle.clearablePct, 20.9, '41770/200000 — a conta que ANTES dava 594%');\n  routerServer.resetMetrics();\n});\n\ntest('metricsSnapshot: sem prompt medido, clearablePct e null (nao finge 0% nem 594%)', () => {\n  routerServer.resetMetrics();\n  assertEq(routerServer.metricsSnapshot().cacheCycle.clearablePct, null, 'sem dado, sem numero');\n  // estado LEGADO (v2.20.2: so tem inputTokens, sem promptTokens) nao pode gerar % errado\n  routerServer.metricsCacheCycle({\n    state: { lastCacheState: 'miss' }, boundary: { cold: true, reason: 'no-state' },\n    prize: { rebuiltTokens: 5000, clearableTokens: 3000 },\n  });\n  assertEq(routerServer.metricsSnapshot().cacheCycle.clearablePct, null,\n    'sem denominador confiavel, prefere NAO responder a responder errado');\n  routerServer.resetMetrics();\n});\n\n\n// Medição real: 23 fronteiras, media de ~7K de input cada. Pequeno demais p/ uma\n// sessao acumulada — assinatura de que a maioria e `no-state` (turno 0 de sessao\n// NOVA, onde nao ha nada acumulado p/ limpar). O alvo da limpeza e a OUTRA\n// fronteira: a sessao LONGA que esfriou (`gap-expired`), com 100K+ de tool_result\n// parado. Somar as duas numa media unica esconde justamente o caso que decide, e\n// decidir pela media mataria a fase seguinte pelo motivo errado.\n\ntest('metricsCacheCycle: separa fronteira de sessao NOVA da que ESFRIOU', () => {\n  routerServer.resetMetrics();\n  const m0 = routerServer.metricsSnapshot();\n  assertEq(typeof m0.cacheCycle.byReason, 'object', 'baseline por motivo existe desde o boot');\n  assertEq(m0.cacheCycle.byReason['no-state'].count, 0);\n  assertEq(m0.cacheCycle.byReason['gap-expired'].count, 0);\n  assertEq(m0.cacheCycle.byReason['prior-miss'].count, 0);\n\n  // turno 0 de sessao nova: quase nada acumulado\n  routerServer.metricsCacheCycle({\n    state: { lastCacheState: 'miss' }, boundary: { cold: true, reason: 'no-state' },\n    prize: { inputTokens: 3000, clearableTokens: 200 },\n  });\n  // sessao LONGA que esfriou: o caso que a limpeza existe p/ atacar\n  routerServer.metricsCacheCycle({\n    state: { lastCacheState: 'miss' }, boundary: { cold: true, reason: 'gap-expired' },\n    prize: { inputTokens: 140000, clearableTokens: 68000 },\n  });\n  const m = routerServer.metricsSnapshot();\n  assertEq(m.cacheCycle.byReason['no-state'].clearableTokens, 200);\n  assertEq(m.cacheCycle.byReason['gap-expired'].clearableTokens, 68000);\n  assertEq(m.cacheCycle.coldBoundaryClearableTokens, 68200, 'o total continua batendo com a soma');\n  routerServer.resetMetrics();\n});\n\ntest('metricsCacheCycle: motivo desconhecido nao derruba nem inventa bucket', () => {\n  routerServer.resetMetrics();\n  routerServer.metricsCacheCycle({\n    state: { lastCacheState: 'miss' }, boundary: { cold: true, reason: 'motivo-que-nao-existe' },\n    prize: { inputTokens: 500, clearableTokens: 100 },\n  });\n  const m = routerServer.metricsSnapshot();\n  assertEq(m.cacheCycle.coldBoundaries, 1, 'a fronteira continua contada');\n  assertEq(Object.keys(m.cacheCycle.byReason).length, 3, 'nao cria bucket novo a partir de entrada externa');\n  routerServer.resetMetrics();\n});\n\n// CALIBRAÇÃO IN-BAND: o `clearableTokens` e estimativa (chars/4). O fator real\n// nao precisa de API key nem de /count_tokens: o proxy ja ve, no MESMO ponto, o\n// total de input REAL que a Anthropic reportou e o corpo que ele mediu em chars.\n// A razao entre os dois E o chars-por-token, medido em trafego real, de graca.\n\ntest('estimateTotalChars: mede o corpo INTEIRO (denominador da calibracao)', () => {\n  const body = {\n    system: 's'.repeat(100),\n    messages: [\n      { role: 'user', content: 'u'.repeat(200) },\n      { role: 'assistant', content: [{ type: 'text', text: 't'.repeat(300) }] },\n    ],\n  };\n  const chars = inventar');\n});\n\ntest('estimateTotalChars: system em BLOCOS (shape real do Claude Code) tambem conta', () => {\n  const chars = cacheCycle.estimateTotalChars({\n    system: [{ type: 'text', text: 'x'.repeat(500) }], messages: [],\n  });\n  assertEq(chars >= 500, true, 'o system e a maior parte do prefixo; ignora-lo viciaria o fator, got ' + chars);\n});\n\ntest('calibrationSample: razao chars/token a partir do usage REAL da resposta', () => {\n  // 40000 chars de corpo contra 10000 tokens reais → 4.0 chars/token\n  const s = cacheCycle.calibrationSample(40000, { in: 2000, cacheRead: 5000, cacheCreate: 3000 });\n  assertEq(s.realTokens, 10000, 'input real = in + cacheRead + cacheCreate');\n  assertEq(s.chars, 40000);\n  assertEq(s.ratio, 4);\n});\n\ntest('calibrationSample: sem usage utilizavel → null (nao fabrica fator)', () => {\n  assertEq(cacheCycle.calibrationSample(40000, { in: 0, cacheRead: 0, cacheCreate: 0 }), null);\n  assertEq(cacheCycle.calibrationSample(0, { in: amostras', () => {\n  routerServer.resetMetrics();\n  const m0 = routerServer.metricsSnapshot();\n  assertEq(m0.calibration.chars, 0, 'baseline desde o boot');\n  assertEq(m0.calibration.realTokens, 0);\n  assertEq(m0.calibration.charsPerToken, null, 'sem amostra, NAO finge um fator');\n\n  routerServer.metricsCalibration({ chars: 40000, realTokens: 10000, ratio: 4 });\n  routerServer.metricsCalibration({ chars: 21000, realTokens: 6000, ratio: 3.5 });\n  const m = routerServer.metricsSnapshot();\n  assertEq(m.calibration.samples, 2);\n  assertEq(m.calibration.chars, 61000);\n  assertEq(m.calibration.realTokens, 16000);\n  // agregado (61000/16000 = 3.8125), NAO a media das razoes — amostra grande pesa mais\n  assertEq(m.calibration.charsPerToken, 3.81);\n  routerServer.resetMetrics();\n});\n\ntest('metricsCalibration: amostra invalida (null) e ignorada sem sujar o agregado', () => {\n  routerServer.resetMetrics();\n  routerServer.metricsCalibration(null);\n  routerServer.metricsCalibration({ chars: 8000, realTokens: 2000, ratio: 4 });\n  const m = routerServer.metricsSnapshot();\n  assertEq(m.calibration.samples, 1);\n  assertEq(m.calibration.charsPerToken, 4);\n  routerServer.resetMetrics();\n});\n\n\ntest('observeCacheCycle: o mapa de sessões tem TETO DURO (proxy longevo não vaza)', () => {\n  const states = new Map();\n  // 5100 sessões distintas, TODAS recentes: a poda por idade sozinha não remove\n  // nada, então sem um teto duro o mapa cresceria sem limite.\n  for (let i = 0; i < 5100; i++) {\n    routerServer.observeCacheCycle('s' + i, { cacheRead: 1 }, 1000 + i, {}, { states });\n  }\n  assertEq(states.size <= 5000, true, 'tamanho ficou em ' + states.size);\n  assertEq(states.has('s5099'), true, 'a sessão mais RECENTE nunca é a descartada');\n});\n\n\n// ─── model-router (server): sticky-tier — chave de sessão + decisor puro ──────\n\ntest('computeSessionKey: mesmo system+1ª msg → MESMA chave (histórico cresce no fim)', () => {\n  const t0 = { system: 'S', messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'first' }] };\n  const t1 = { system: 'S', messages: [\n    { role: 'user', content: 'first' },\n    { role: 'assistant', content: 'reply' },\n    { role: 'user', content: 'second turn' },\n  ] };\n  assertEq(routerServer.computeSessionKey(t0), routerServer.computeSessionKey(t1));\n});\n\ntest('computeSessionKey: 1ª msg diferente → chave DIFERENTE', () => {\n  const a = { system: 'S', messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'first' }] };\n  const b = { system: 'S', messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'DIFFERENT' }] };\n  assert(routerServer.computeSessionKey(a) !== routerServer.computeSessionKey(b), 'chaves devem diferir');\n});\n\ntest('computeSessionKey: system em array de blocos é normalizado p/ texto', () => {\n  const asStr = { system: 'Hello\\nWorld', messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'q' }] };\n  const asArr = { system: [{ type: 'text', text: 'Hello' }, { type: 'text', text: 'World' }], messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'q' }] };\n  assertEq(routerServer.computeSessionKey(asStr), routerServer.computeSessionKey(asArr));\n});\n\ntest('computeSessionKey: sem system/messages não quebra (sha1 hex) + content em blocos', () => {\n  const k = routerServer.computeSessionKey({});\n });\n  assert(typeof kBlocks === 'string' && kBlocks.length === 40, 'content em blocos vira texto');\n});\n\ntest('decideStickyModel: 1ª call classifica+fixa; 2ª REUSA sem reclassificar; TTL → re-pin', async () => {\n  const config = { sticky: { enabled: true, ttlMs: 1000 }, routing: { ceiling: true, catalog: { enabled: false } } };\n  const pins = new Map();\n  let calls = 0;\n  const classifyFn = async () => { calls += 1; return 'haiku'; };\n  const body0 = { model: 'claude-sonnet-4-6', system: 'sys', messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'hi' }] };\n\n  const d1 = await routerServer.decideStickyModel(body0, config, { pins, now: 1000, classifyFn });\n  assertEq(calls, 1);            // classificou uma vez (turno 0)\n  assertEq(d1.created, true);    // pin criado\n  assertEq(d1.pinned, true);\n  assertEq(d1.tier, 'haiku');\n  assert(d1.model.includes('haiku'), 'modelo fixado no haiku');\n\n  // 2ª request da MESMA sessão (mais mensagens, mesmo prefixo) → mesma chave.\n  const body1 = { model: 'claude-sonnet-4-6', system: 'sys', messages: [\n    { role: 'user', content: 'hi' },\n    { role: 'assistant', content: 'yo' },\n    { role: 'user', content: 'again' },\n  ] };\n  const d2 = await routerServer.decideStickyModel(body1, config, { pins, now: 1500, classifyFn });\n  assertEq(calls, 1);            // NÃO reclassificou (cache-safe)\n  assertEq(d2.created, false);   // pin reusado\n  assertEq(d2.tier, d1.tier);    // MESMO tier\n  assertEq(d2.model, d1.model);  // MESMO modelo (cache preservado)\n\n  // Após o TTL (now > expiresAt), re-pina (classifica de novo).\n  const d3 = await routerServer.decideStickyModel(body1, config, { pins, now: 5000, classifyFn });\n  assertEq(calls, 2);            // reclassificou após expiração\n  assertEq(d3.created, true);\n});\n\ntest('decideStickyModel: teto respeita o /model ATUAL (classificou opus, escolheu sonnet → sonnet)', async () => {\n  const config = { sticky: { enabled: true, 'sys', messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'design a system' }] };\n  const d = await routerServer.decideStickyModel(body, config, { pins, now: 1000, classifyFn });\n  // O teto já rebaixa o acima do escolhido\n  assertEq(d.model, 'claude-sonnet-4-6');\n  assertEq(d.blocked, false);    // pinnedTier já é sonnet → nada a barrar na reaplicação\n});\n\ntest('decideStickyModel: usuário assertEq(srRetrieve.applyGate(es, { minScore: 0.5, topK: 5 }).length, 2);\n});\n\ntest('self-review-retrieve.readDaemonPort/Token: absent → null', () => {\n  const dir = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'ccb-sr-nolock-'));\n  assertEq(srRetrieve.readDaemonPort(dir), null);\n  const savedTok = process.env.BRAIN_HTTP_TOKEN; delete process.env.BRAIN_HTTP_TOKEN;\n  assertEq(srRetrieve.readDaemonToken(dir), null);\n  if (savedTok !== undefined) process.env.BRAIN_HTTP_TOKEN = savedTok;\n});\n\n/** Fake warm brain-server HTTP daemon: token-gated /mcp with initialize→tools/call. */\nfunction startFakeBrainHttp({ results = [], toolError = false } = {}) {\n  const seen = { auth: null, calledTool: null, callArgs: null };\n  const server = http.createServer((req, res) => {\n    seen.auth = req.headers['authorization'] || null;\n    let body = '';\n    req.on('data', c => { body += c; });\n    req.on('end', () => {\n      let msg = {}; try { msg = JSON.parse(body); } catch (err) { void err; }\n      if (msg.method === 'initialize') {\n        res.writeHead(200, { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Mcp-Session-Id': 'sess-1' });\n        return res.end(JSON.stringify({ jsonrpc: '2.0', id: msg.id, result: { protocolVersion: '2025-06-18', capabilities: {} } }));\n      }\n      if (msg.method === 'notifications/initialized') { res.writeHead(202); return res.end(); }\n      if (msg.method === 'tools/call') {\n        seen.calledTool = msg.params && msg.params.name;\n        seen.callArgs = msg.params && msg.params.arguments;\n        res.writeHead(200, { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' });\n        // Tool-level failure: a SUCCESSFUL JSON-RPC response with result.isError.\n        const result = toolError\n          ? { isError: true, content: [{ type: 'text', text: 'brain_search failed: boom' }] }\n          : { content: [{ type: 'text', text: JSON.stringify({ results }) }] };\n        return res.end(JSON.stringify({ jsonrpc: '2.0', id: msg.id, result }));\n      }\n      res.writeHead(400); res.end('bad');\n    });\n  });\n  return new Promise(resolve => {\n    server.listen(0, '127.0.0.1', () => resolve({\n      port: server.address().port, seen, close: () => new Promise(r => server.close(r)),\n    }));\n  });\n}\n\ntest('self-review-retrieve.retrieveViaDaemon: token handshake + brain_search parse', async () => {\n  const daemon = await startFakeBrainHttp({ results: [{ id: 'x', title: 'past bug', type: 'lesson', score: 0.8, recurrence: 2 }] });\n  const dir = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'ccb-sr-daemon-'));\n  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(dir, 'brain-http.lock.json'), JSON.stringify({ port: daemon.port }));\n  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(dir, 'brain-http.token'), 'tok');\n  const savedTok = process.env.BRAIN_HTTP_TOKEN; delete process.env.BRAIN_HTTP_TOKEN;\n  try {\n    const entries = await srRetrieve.retrieveViaDaemon('hooks config', { dataDir: dir, project: 'p1', topK: 2, minScore: 0.2, timeoutMs: 3000 });\n    assert(Array.isArray(entries), 'entries array');\n    assertEq(entries[0].id, 'x');\n    assert(String(daemon.seen.auth || '').includes('tok'), 'daemon received bearer token');\n    assertEq(daemon.seen.calledTool, 'brain_search');\n  } finally {\n    if (savedTok !== undefined) process.env.BRAIN_HTTP_TOKEN = savedTok;\n    await daemon.close();\n  }\n});\n\ntest('self-review-retrieve.retrieveViaDaemon: no lock/token → null (fall back)', async () => {\n  const dir = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'ccb-sr-nodaemon-'));\n  const savedTok = process.env.BRAIN_HTTP_TOKEN; delete process.env.BRAIN_HTTP_TOKEN;\n  try {\n    assertEq(await srRetrieve.retrieveViaDaemon('q', { dataDir: dir, project: 'p', topK: 2 }), null);\n  } finally { if (savedTok !== undefined) process.env.BRAIN_HTTP_TOKEN = savedTok; }\n});\n\ntest('self-review-retrieve.retrieveViaDaemon: tool-level error (isError) → null (fall back)', async () => {\n  const daemon = await startFakeBrainHttp({ toolError: true });\n  const dir = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'ccb-sr-toolerr-'));\n  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(dir, 'brain-http.lock.json'), JSON.stringify({ port: daemon.port }));\n  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(dir, 'brain-http.token'), 'tok');\n  const savedTok = process.env.BRAIN_HTTP_TOKEN; delete process.env.BRAIN_HTTP_TOKEN;\n  try {\n    // A reachable daemon whose brain_search errors must return null (not []), so\n    // retrieve() degrades to the keyword index rather than silently yielding nothing.\n    assertEq(await srRetrieve.retrieveViaDaemon('q', { dataDir: dir, project: 'p1', topK: 2, minScore: 0.2, timeoutMs: 3000 }), null);\n  } finally {\n    if (savedTok !== undefined) process.env.BRAIN_HTTP_TOKEN = savedTok;\n    await daemon.close();\n  }\n});\n\ntest('self-review-retrieve.retrieveViaIndex: lookup → store.get wiring + score merge', async () => {\n  // In-memory fakes via DI: verifies the orchestration (index.lookup → per-hit\n  // store.get → attach score) without touching the real singletons/fs, so it's\n  // immune to concurrent-test races. Real brain-index/brain-store are covered\n  // by their own tests.\n  const fakeIndex = {\n    init: async () => {},\n    lookup: async (kw) => (kw.includes('profile') ? [{ id: 'les1', score: 0.9 }, { id: 'missing', score: 0.4 }] : []),\n  };\n  const store = {\n    les1: { id: 'les1', title: 'profile deepMerge alias', type: 'lesson', recurrence: 3 },\n  };\n  const fakeStore = { init: async () => {}, get: async (id) => store[id] || null };\n  const got = await srRetrieve.retrieveViaIndex(['profile', 'merge', 'alias'], { project: 'p1', topK: 2, _store: fakeStore, _index: fakeIndex });\n  assert(got.some(x => x.id === 'les1'), 'seeded lesson retrieved');\n  assertEq(got.find(x => x.id === 'les1').recurrence, 3);\n  assertEq(got.find(x => x.id === 'les1').score, 0.9); // score attached from the hit\n  assert(!got.some(x => x.id === 'missing'), 'hits with no store entry are dropped');\n});\n\ntest('self-review.buildQuery: basenames + non-generic dirs, split keywords', () => {\n  const q = selfReview.buildQuery(['scripts/lib/hooks-config.js', 'C:\\\\x\\\\verify-nudge.js', 'src/index.js']);\n  assert(q.query.includes('hooks-config.js'), 'basename kept');\n  assert(q.query.includes('verify-nudge.js'), 'windows path basename kept');\n  assert(!q.query.split(' ').includes('src'), 'generic dir dropped');\n  assert(q.keywords.includes('hooks') && q.keywords.includes('config'), 'keywords split on punctuation');\n});\n\ntest('self-review.buildQuery: relativizes against cwd, dropping OS/user/container noise (regression)', () => {\n  // Live bug: edited paths arrive ABSOLUTE (file-edit-detect.js journals\n  // tool_input.file_path as-is). Without relativizing against cwd first, every\n  // real invocation walks the WHOLE absolute path — \"Users\"/\"<username>\"/\n  // \"Desktop\"/\"Projetos\"/\"<repo>\" are never in GENERIC_DIR and become query\n  // noise on every turn, diluting the keyword-index match score (matched/total\n  // fraction) below the shared relevance gate and silently starving the\n  // embedder-free fallback path. Confirmed live: a 12-keyword noisy query\n  // scored 0.166 (2/12) and was gated out at minScore 0.2; the same edit\n  // relativized against cwd scored 1.0 (2/2) and passed.\n  const cwd = 'C:\\\\Users\\\\dev\\\\Desktop\\\\Projetos\\\\myrepo';\n  const q = selfReview.buildQuery([`${cwd}\\\\scripts\\\\oneoff-store.js`], cwd);\n  assertEq(q.query, 'oneoff-store.js', 'only the basename remains once cwd noise is stripped');\n  assert(!q.keywords.some(k => ['users', 'dev', 'desktop', 'projetos', 'myrepo'].includes(k)),\n    'no OS/user/container path segments leak into keywords');\n  assert(q.keywords.includes('oneoff') && q.keywords.includes('store'), 'the real keywords survive');\n});\n\ntest('self-review.buildQuery: path outside cwd (or no cwd given) keeps the prior absolute-segments behavior', () => {\n  const cwd = 'C:\\\\Users\\\\dev\\\\Desktop\\\\Projetos\\\\myrepo';\n  const outside = selfReview.buildQuery(['C:\\\\other\\\\place\\\\oneoff-store.js'], cwd);\n  assert(outside.query.includes('other') && outside.query.includes('place'),\n    'a path outside cwd is NOT relativized — falls back to the old behavior, not worse than before');\n  const noCwd = selfReview.buildQuery(['scripts/lib/hooks-config.js']);\n  assert(noCwd.query.includes('hooks-config.js'), 'omitting cwd entirely still works (backward compatible)');\n});\n\ntest('self-review.buildQuery: cwd/file_path case mismatch still relativizes on win32 (adversarial review finding)', () => {\n  // A case-sensitive startsWith would silently miss this and fall through to\n  // the exact noise-pollution bug the fix exists for — with zero signal.\n  if (process.platform !== 'win32') return; // POSIX case genuinely distinguishes files; not applicable\n  const cwd = 'c:\\\\Users\\\\dev\\\\Desktop\\\\Projetos\\\\myrepo';\n  const q = selfReview.buildQuery(['C:\\\\USERS\\\\DEV\\\\Desktop\\\\Projetos\\\\MyRepo\\\\scripts\\\\oneoff-store.js'], cwd);\n  assertEq(q.query, 'oneoff-store.js', 'relativizes despite differing case in drive letter and path segments');\n});\n\ntest('self-review.buildQuery: root cwd (\"/\") still relativizes (empty-normCwd edge case)', () => {\n  const q = absent');\n});\n\ntest('self-review.buildAdvisory: [SELF-REVIEW] header + recurrence + type', () => {\n  const a = selfReview.buildAdvisory([{ title: 'Broke the build', type: 'lesson', recurrence: 4 omitted');\n});\n\ntest('self-review.run: edits + retrieved lesson → block, journals, dedups next turn', async () => {\n  // Uses the top-level journal singletons (bound to the units temp data dir) with\n  // a UNIQUE sid + a STUB retrieve (DI) → no cache/env swapping, no store race.\n  const sid = 'srsession-' + Date.now();\n  const cwd = path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'srproj');\n  const dir = process.env.CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA; // where the top-level journals live\n  const stubRetrieve = async () => ({ entries: [{ id: 'lesX', title: 'widget parser off-by-one', type: 'lesson', recurrence: 2, score: 0.8 }], source: 'index' });\n  const noopMetrics = { fire: () => {} }; // don't touch the shared brain-store singleton in-process\n  const srCfg = { enabled: true, topK: 2, minScore: 0.2, await selfReview.run({ hook_event_name: 'Stop', session_id: sid, cwd }, { retrieve: stubRetrieve, dataDir: dir, metrics: noopMetrics, cfg: srCfg });\n  assertEq(first.block, true);\n  assert(String(first.reason).includes('[SELF-REVIEW]') && first.reason.includes('widget parser off-by-one'), 'advisory content');\n\n  // Injection journaled with the self-review tool tag (F3 precision }).status, 'ok');\n  const w = doctor.checkDataDirs({ env: { data: '/a' }, dataDirCandidates: [{ path: '/a', populated: true }, { path: '/b', populated: true }] });\n  assertEq(w.status, 'warn');\n  assert(w.detail.includes('/b'), 'lists the fragmented dir');\n});\n\ntest('doctor.findDataDirCandidates: real fragmentation shape (sibling dirs by prefix)', () => {\n  // Regression: install modes produce SIBLING dirs directly under\n  // .../plugins/data/ named by prefix (claude-code-boss-inline,\n  // claude-code-boss-<marketplace>) — NOT nested under a marketplace folder.\n  // A prior version scanned .../plugins/<mkt>/claude-code-boss and NEVER found\n  // the real fragmentation (caught live by smoke/doctor.mjs).\n  const home = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'ccb-doctor-frag-'));\n  const dataBase = path.join(home, '.claude', 'plugins', 'data');\n  const dirs = ['claude-code-boss', 'claude-code-boss-inline', 'claude-code-boss-allansantos-plugins'];\n  for (const d of dirs) {\n    fs.mkdirSync(path.join(dataBase, d, 'brain', 'someproj'), { recursive: true });\n    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(dataBase, d, 'brain', 'someproj', 'brain.db'), '');\n  }\n  const homeVar = process.platform === 'win32' ? 'USERPROFILE' : 'HOME';\n  const prevHome = process.env[homeVar];\n  process.env[homeVar] = home;\n  try {\n    const active = path.join(dataBase, 'claude-code-boss-inline');\n    const candidates = doctor.findDataDirCandidates(active);\n    const populatedPaths = candidates.filter(c => c.populated).map(c => c.path).sort();\n    assertEq(populatedPaths.length, 3);\n    for (const d of dirs) assert(populatedPaths.includes(path.join(dataBase, d)), `missing ${d}`);\n  } finally {\n    if (prevHome === undefined) delete process.env[homeVar]; else process.env[homeVar] = prevHome;\n    fs.rmSync(home, { recursive: true, force: true });\n  }\n});\n\ntest('data-dir: a VALID env dir yields to the heavier live folder named by the pointer', () => {\n  // Regression (the 401 loop): Claude Code gives each launch mode its own sibling\n  // identity, so two contexts BOTH get a valid-but-different CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA\n  // (-inline for dev/--plugin-dir, -<marketplace> for a marketplace install).\n  // writeActivePointer already refused to move the pointer onto the empty\n  // marketplace dir, but dataDir() returned that empty dir anyway — so consumers\n  // looked for brain-http.token where there was none and every daemon call 401'd.\n  // Consolidation can never repair this: it only absorbs POPULATED siblings.\n  // NOTE: kept SYNC on purpose — it mutates process.env, and this runner runs\n  // async tests concurrently (an async version would race sibling tests).\n  const home = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'ccb-ddir-split-'));\n  const dataBase = path.join(home, '.claude', 'plugins', 'data');\n  const live = path.join(dataBase, 'claude-code-boss-inline');\n  const stray = path.join(dataBase, 'claude-code-boss-allansantos-plugins');\n  // `live` holds the KB (heavier); `stray` exists but is empty (no brain/, no token).\n  fs.mkdirSync(path.join(live, 'brain', 'proj'), { recursive: true });\n  fs.writeFileSync(path.join(live, 'brain', 'proj', 'brain.db'), 'x'.repeat(4096));\n  fs.mkdirSync(stray, { recursive: true });\n\n  const homeVar = process.platform === 'win32' ? 'USERPROFILE' : 'HOME';\n  const prevHome = process.env[homeVar];\n  const prevData = process.env.CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA;\n  process.env[homeVar] = home;\n  try {\n    const dd = require('./lib/data-dir.js');\n    dd.writeActivePointer(live);                    // the live folder publishes itself\n    assertEq(dd.readActivePointer(), live);\n\n    // A marketplace-launched consumer arrives with a VALID env pointing at the\n    // empty sibling → must FOLLOW the live folder, not strand itself.\n    process.env.CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA = stray;\n    assertEq(dd.dataDir(), live);\n    assertEq(dd.readActivePointer(), live);         // pointer must NOT regress\n\n    // Symmetry: the heavier folder's own env still resolves to itself.\n    process.env.CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA = live;\n    assertEq(dd.dataDir(), live);\n\n    // No regression when there is nothing heavier: an env dir wins on a machine\n    // whose siblings are all weightless (fresh install must not be hijacked).\n    fs.rmSync(path.join(live, 'brain'), { recursive: true, force: true });\n    process.env.CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA = stray;\n    assertEq(dd.dataDir(), stray);\n  } finally {\n    if (prevHome === undefined) delete process.env[homeVar]; else process.env[homeVar] = prevHome;\n    if (prevData === undefined) delete process.env.CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA; else process.env.CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA = prevData;\n    fs.rmSync(home, { recursive: true, force: true });\n  }\n});\n\ntest('doctor.checkModel: present → ok, absent → warn', () => {\n  assertEq(doctor.checkModel({ modelPresent: true, modelCacheDir: '/m' }).status, 'ok');\n  assertEq(doctor.checkModel({ modelPresent: false }).status, 'warn');\n});\n\ntest('doctor.checkDaemon: no lock → ok, unhealthy lock → warn, healthy → ok', () => {\n  assertEq(doctor.checkDaemon({ daemon: { lockPresent: false } }).status, 'ok');\n  assertEq(doctor.checkDaemon({ daemon: { lockPresent: true, healthy: true, tokenReadable: true, port: 5 } }).status, 'ok');\n  assertEq(doctor.checkDaemon({ daemon: { lockPresent: true, healthy: false } }).status, 'warn');\n  assertEq(doctor.checkDaemon({ daemon: { lockPresent: true, healthy: true, tokenReadable: false } }).status, 'warn');\n});\n\ntest('doctor.checkHooksEvents: evento OFICIAL nao pode virar falso positivo (report de campo)', () => {\n  // Report de campo (v2.19.0): o doctor gritou `[x] unrecognized event(s): SubagentStart`\n  // e mandou \"corrigir o nome em hooks/hooks.json\". SubagentStart e um evento OFICIAL\n  // e documentado (\"When a subagent is spawned\"), usado pelo plugin p/ injetar as\n  // politicas no subagente. () => ([\n  { id: 'a', type: 'lesson', recurrence: 2, confidence: 0.9, createdAt: '2026-01-01', vector: [1] },\n  { id: 'b', type: 'lesson', recurrence: 5, confidence: 0.8, createdAt: '2026-01-02', '2026-01-01', vector: [1] },\n  { id: 'e', type: 'lesson', recurrence: 3, confidence: 0.5, createdAt: '2026-01-04', vector: null },\n]);\nconst _clusterSim = (a, b) => (a[0] === b[0] ? 0.8 : 0.2);\n\ntest('consolidate-plan.planMerges: groups same-type near-dupes, sums recurrence', () => {\n  const plans = consolidatePlan.planMerges(_fakeEntries(), _clusterSim, { minSim: 0.7, maxSim: 0.9 });\n  assertEq(plans.length, 1);\n  assertEq(plans[0].survivorId, 'b'); // highest recurrence\n  assertEq(plans[0].absorbedIds, ['a']);\n  assertEq(plans[0].newRecurrence, 7); // 2 + 5\n  assertEq(plans[0].type, 'lesson');\n});\n\ntest('consolidate-plan.planMerges: respects type boundary + similarity band + null vectors', () => {\n  const plans = consolidatePlan.planMerges(_fakeEntries(), _clusterSim, { minSim: 0.7, maxSim: 0.9 });\n  const touched = new Set([plans[0].survivorId, ...plans[0].absorbedIds]);\n  assert(!touched.has('d'), 'pattern d not merged with lessons');\n  assert(!touched.has('c'), 'different-cluster c not merged');\n  assert(!touched.has('e'), 'null-vector e excluded');\n});\n\ntest('consolidate-plan.planMerges: sim above maxSim (exact dup) is NOT merged here', () => {\n  // maxSim gate: identical (sim=1.0) entries are out of the [0.7,0.9] band.\n  const sim = () => 1.0;\n  const es = [\n    { id: 'x', type: 'lesson', recurrence: 1, vector: [1] },\n    { id: 'y', type: 'lesson', recurrence: 1, vector: [1] },\n  ];\n  assertEq(consolidatePlan.planMerges(es, sim, { minSim: 0.7, maxSim: 0.9 }).length, 0);\n});\n\ntest('consolidate-plan.planMerges: survivor-anchored — chained-but-dissimilar entry is NOT absorbed', () => {\n  // A~B and B~C in band, but A~C below minSim. Single-linkage would group all\n  // three; survivor-anchored must NOT absorb the entry dissimilar to the survivor.\n  const sim = (a, b) => {\n    const k = a[0] + '-' + b[0], k2 = b[0] + '-' + a[0];\n    const band = { 'A-B': 0.85, 'B-A': 0.85, 'B-C': 0.85, 'C-B': 0.85 };\n    return band[k] || band[k2] || 0.4; // A-C = 0.4 (below minSim)\n  };\n  const es = [\n    { id: 'A', type: 'lesson', recurrence: 9, confidence: 0.9, createdAt: '2026-01-01', vector: ['A'] },\n    { id: 'B', type: 'lesson', recurrence: 2, confidence: 0.5, createdAt: '2026-01-02', vector: ['B'] },\n    { id: 'C', type: 'lesson', recurrence: 1, confidence: 0.5, createdAt: '2026-01-03', vector: ['C'] },\n  ];\n  const plans = consolidatePlan.planMerges(es, sim, { minSim: 0.7, maxSim: 0.9 });\n  assertEq(plans.length, 1);\n  assertEq(plans[0].survivorId, 'A'); // highest recurrence\n  assertEq(plans[0].absorbedIds, ['B']); // B is in-band with A; C (0.4 to A) is NOT\n  assert(!plans[0].absorbedIds.includes('C'), 'dissimilar C must not be deleted');\n  assertEq(plans[0].newRecurrence, 11); // 9 + 2 (C excluded)\n});\n\ntest('brain-consolidate.consolidate: dry-run plans, --apply merges atomically (fake store)', async () => {\n  const state = { entries: _fakeEntries(), applied: [] };\n  const fakeStore = {\n    init: async () => {}, getStorageType: () => 'sqlite',\n    cosineSimilarity: _clusterSim,\n    listWithVectors: () => state.entries,\n    applyConsolidation: (survivorId, newRecurrence, absorbedIds) => {\n      state.applied.push({ survivorId, newRecurrence, absorbedIds });\n      return { ok: true, deleted: absorbedIds.length };\n    },\n  };\n  const dry = await brainConsolidate.consolidate({ project: 'p', apply: false, _store: fakeStore });\n  assertEq(dry.groups, 1);\n  assertEq(dry.merged, 0);\n  assertEq(state.applied.length, 0); // dry run mutates nothing\n  const applied = await brainConsolidate.consolidate({ project: 'p', apply: true, _store: fakeStore });\n  assertEq(applied.merged, 1);\n  assertEq(applied.deleted, 1);\n  assertEq(state.applied[0].survivorId, 'b');\n  assertEq(state.applied[0].newRecurrence, 7);\n  assertEq(state.applied[0].absorbedIds, ['a']);\n});\n\ntest('brain-consolidate.consolidate: non-sqlite store → no-op', async () => {\n  const fakeStore = { init: async () => {}, getStorageType: () => 'json' };\n  const r = await brainConsolidate.consolidate({ project: 'p', apply: true, _store: fakeStore });\n  assertEq(r.reason, 'not-sqlite');\n  assertEq(r.groups, 0);\n});\n\ntest('value-summary.summarize: retrieval precision (cited / injected)', () => {\n  const s = valueSummary.summarize([\n    { eventName: 'retrieve.injected', ts: 1, payload: { count: 10 } },\n    { eventName: 'retrieve.injected', ts: 2, payload: { count: 10 } },\n    { eventName: 'retrieve.cited', ts: 1, payload: { entryId: 'x' } },\n    { eventName: 'retrieve.cited', ts: 2, payload: { entryId: 'y' } },\n  ]);\n  assertEq(s.retrievalPrecision.injected, 20);\n  assertEq(s.retrievalPrecision.cited, 2);\n  assertEq(s.retrievalPrecision.rate, 0.1);\n  assertEq(valueSummary.summarize([]).retrievalPrecision.rate, 0); // no injections → 0, no div-by-zero\n});\n\n// ─── stop-dispatcher: merge + priority ───────────────────────────────────────\nconst {});\n});\n\ntest('stop-dispatcher.mergeBlocks: single block → {decision:block, reason}', () => {\n  assertEq(dispatcher.mergeBlocks([{ name: 'auto-continue-stop', reason: 'go' }]),\n    { decision: 'block', reason: 'go' });\n});\n\ntest('stop-dispatcher.mergeBlocks: 2 blocks concatenate with the separator', () => {\n  const out = dispatcher.mergeBlocks([\n    { name: 'pattern-detect', reason: assertEq(dispatcher.rank('curation-stop'), 0);\n  assertEq(dispatcher.rank('failure-retro'), 1);\n  assertEq(dispatcher.rank('pattern-detect'), 2);\n  assertEq(dispatcher.rank('anything'), 2);\n});\n\ntest('stop-dispatcher.DETECTORS: 16 detectors, ordering invariants hold', () => {\n  const names = dispatcher.DETECTORS.map(d => d.name);\n  assertEq(names.length, 16);\n 'session-summary (U2) registered');\n  assert(names.includes('conversation-ingest'), 'conversation-ingest (GAP1) registered');\n  assert(names.includes('capture-dispatch'), 'capture-dispatch (Phase 1 capture) registered');\n  assert(names.indexOf('self-review') < names.indexOf('verify-nudge'),\n    'self-review must read verify-journal before verify-nudge clears it');\n  assert(names.indexOf('failure-retro') < names.indexOf('curation-stop'),\n    'failure-retro must run before curation-stop (defer-before-clear)');\n  assert(names.indexOf('decision-scan-response') < detector exposes run()');\n});\n\n// ─── stop-telemetry + dispatcher gate model (Phase 1 observability) ──────────\nconst telem = require('./lib/stop-telemetry.js');\n\ntest('stop-telemetry.gateState: chars: 20, ms: 2 },\n    { name: 'e', gated: true, blocked: false, would_block: false, chars: 0, ms: 2 },\n  ]);\n  assertEq(s.profile, 'standard');\n  assertEq(s.run_id, 'run1');\n  assertEq(s.schema, telem.SCHEMA_VERSION);\n  assertEq(s.evaluated, 5);\n  assertEq(s.blocked, 1);\n  assertEq(s.gated, 3);\n  assertEq(s.shadow, 2);\n  assertEq(s.enforcedChars, 10);\n  assertEq(s.avoidedChars, 20);\n  assertEq(s.detectors.find(x => x.name === 'a').s, 'block');\n  assertEq(s.detectors.find(x => x.name === 'c').s, 'gated');\n  assertEq(s.detectors.find(x => x.name === 'd').s, 'shadow_block');\n  assertEq(s.detectors.find(x => x.name === 'e').s, 'shadow_quiet');\n});\n\ntest('stop-dispatcher.dispatch: enabled detector runs + blocks (injected)', async () => {\n  const fake = { name: 'decision-promote', mod: { run: async () => ({ block: true, reason: 'HELLO' }) } };\n  const r = await dispatcher.dispatch({}, { profile: 'dev', runId: 'r', detectors: [fake] true);\n});\n\ntest('stop-dispatcher.dispatch: gated + detect + sampled → shadow would_block, NEVER enforced', async () => {\n  let detectRan = false;\n  const fake = { name: 'refine-research', mod: profile: 'free', runId: 'r', shadowRate: 1, detectors: [fake] });\n  assertEq(detectRan, true);\n  assertEq(r.blocks.length, 0);\n  assertEq(r.detectors[0].would_block, true);\n  assertEq(r.detectors[0].chars, 5);\n});\n\ntest('stop-dispatcher.dispatch: detector error → onError called, no block', async () => {\n  const errs = [];\n  const fake = { name: 'decision-promote', mod: { run: async () => { throw new Error('boom'); } } };\n  const r = await dispatcher.dispatch({}, { profile: 'dev', runId: 'r', detectors: [fake], onError: (n, m) => errs.push([n, m]) });\n  assertEq(r.blocks.length, 0);\n  assertEq(errs.length, 1);\n  assertEq(errs[0][0], 'decision-promote');\n});\n\n// ─── profile-impact aggregator (Phase 2 Insights) ────────────────────────────\nconst profileImpact = require('./lib/profile-impact.js');\n\ntest('profile-impact.aggregate: folds stop.dispatch per profile', () => {\n  const evs = [\n    { payload: { profile: 'standard', blocked: 1, gated: 3, shadow: 1, enforcedChars: 100, avoidedChars: 50,\n      detectors: [\n        { name: 'refine-research', s: 'gated', ms: 0 },\n        { name: 'failure-retro', s: 'shadow_block', ms: 2 },\n        { name: 'auto-continue-stop', s: 'gated', ms: 0 },\n        { name: 'curation-stop', s: 'block', ms: 1 },\n      ] } },\n    { payload: { profile: 'standard', blocked: 0, gated: 2, shadow: 0, enforcedChars: 0, avoidedChars: 0,\n      detectors: [\n        { name: 'refine-research', s: 'gated', ms: 0 },\n        { name: 'failure-retro', s: 'gated', ms: 0 },\n      ] } },\n    { payload: { profile: 'free', blocked: 0, gated: 5, shadow: 0, enforcedChars: 0, avoidedChars: 0, detectors: [] } },\n  ];\n  const r = profileImpact.aggregateProfileImpact(evs);\n  const std = r.profiles.find(p => p.profile === 'standard');\n  assertEq(std.stops, 2);\n  assertEq(std.blocked, 1);\n  assertEq(std.gated, 5);\n  assertEq(std.wouldBlock, 1);\n  assertEq(std.avoidedChars, 50);\n  assertEq(std.topGated[0].name, 'refine-research'); // gated twice → top\n  assertEq(std.topGated[0].count, 2);\n  assertEq(r.profiles[0].profile, 'standard'); // sorted by stops desc\n  assertEq(r.profiles.find(p => p.profile === 'free').gated, 5);\n});\n\ntest('profile-impact.aggregate: empty/garbage tolerated', () => {\n  assertEq(profileImpact.aggregateProfileImpact([]).profiles.length, 0);\n  assertEq(profileImpact.aggregateProfileImpact(null).profiles.length, 0);\n});\n\n// ─── tuning-advisor (mechanical, zero-quota recommendations) ─────────────────\nconst tuningAdvisor = require('./lib/tuning-advisor.js');\n\ntest('tuning-advisor: free with blocks → warn (leak)', () => {\n  const r = tuningAdvisor.analyze({ 'free-leak' && x.level === 'warn'), 'expected free-leak warn');\n});\n\ntest('tuning-advisor: dev with zero blocks → suggest standard', () => {\n  const r = tuningAdvisor.analyze({ activeProfile: [{ profile: 'standard', stops: 30, blocked: 1, gated: 60, wouldBlock: 8, shadowSamples: 10 }] } });\n  assert(hi.recommendations.some(x => x.id === 'standard-costly' && x.level === 'suggest'));\n  const lo = tuningAdvisor.analyze({ activeProfile: 'standard',\n    impact: { profiles: [{ profile: 'standard', stops: 30, blocked: 1, gated: 60, wouldBlock: 1, shadowSamples: 10 }] } });\n  assert(lo.recommendations.some(x => x.id === 'standard-good' && x.level === 'info'));\n});\n\ntest('tuning-advisor: recall precision low → suggest raise minScore', () => {\n  const r = recommends disabling a learning nudge (no nudge-weak)', () => {\n  // Anti-pattern that caused the regression: advising \"the profile could disable it\n  // without loss\" for a low-conversion capture nudge. Low conversion means aim/\n  // surface it better, NOT kill learning. The nudge-weak rule is gone.\n  const r = tuningAdvisor.analyze({ captureRate: { byKind: {\n    correction: { nudges: 50, captures: 0, rate: 0 },\n    pattern: { nudges: 20, captures: 1, rate: 0.05 },\n  } } });\n  assert(!r.recommendations.some(x => String(x.id).startsWith('nudge-weak')),\n    `must not recommend disabling a learning nudge; got ${JSON.stringify(r.recommendations.map(x => x.id))}`);\n});\n\ntest('tuning-advisor: below sample size → silent (no advice on noise)', () => {\n  const r = tuningAdvisor.analyze({ activeProfile: 'dev',\n    impact: { profiles: [{ profile: ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n// Sprint 5 — atomicity + de-duplication (state-write corruption & data-dir split-brain)\n// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n// ─── lib/data-dir.js — one guarded resolver (kills the ${...} split-brain) ────\nconst dataDirLib = require('./lib/data-dir.js');\n\ntest('data-dir: honors a real CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA value AND publishes the active pointer', () => {\n  // (a) env wins — and dataDir() PUBLISHES it to the global pointer so env-less\n  // hooks (SessionStart etc.) can follow the SAME live folder instead of forking.\n  withTempHome((home) => {\n    const saved = process.env.CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA;\n    const real = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'ccb-dd-real-'));\n    try {\n      process.env.CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA = real;\n      assertEq(dataDirLib.dataDir(), real);\n      assertEq(dataDirLib.readActivePointer(), real);\n      assert(fs.existsSync(path.join(home, '.claude', 'claude-code-boss', 'active-data-dir.json')),\n        'dataDir() with a real env must publish the active-data-dir pointer');\n    } finally { process.env.CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA = saved; }\n  });\n});\n\ntest('data-dir: FOLLOWS the published pointer when env is absent', () => {\n  // (b) no env → resolve the app's live folder from the global pointer that an\n  // env-aware process (brain-server / a guarded hook) previously published.\n  withTempHome(() => {\n    const saved = process.env.CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA;\n    const live = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'ccb-dd-live-'));\n    try {\n      delete process.env.CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA;\n      dataDirLib.writeActivePointer(live);\n      assertEq(dataDirLib.dataDir(), live);\n    } finally { process.env.CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA = saved; }\n  });\n});\n\ntest('data-dir: bootstraps the most-recently-written claude-code-boss* sibling (excludes other plugins)', () => {\n  // (c) no env, no pointer → pick the live install by brain/ mtime, and NEVER a\n  // non-claude-code-boss sibling (codex-inline, rf-reviewer-*) even if it is newer.\n  withTempHome((home) => {\n    const saved = process.env.CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA;\n    try {\n      delete process.env.CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA;\n      const base = path.join(home, '.claude', 'plugins', 'data');\n      const mk = (name, mtimeSec) => {\n        const b = path.join(base, name, 'brain');\n        fs.mkdirSync(b, { recursive: true });\n        fs.utimesSync(b, mtimeSec, mtimeSec);\n      };\n      const now = Date.now() / 1000;\n      mk('claude-code-boss', now - 100);\n      mk('claude-code-boss-abcd1234', now - 10);   // most-recent claude-code-boss\n      mk('claude-code-boss-old', now - 500);\n      mk('codex-inline', now + 1000);              // newer, but a DIFFERENT plugin\n      mk('rf-reviewer-xyz', now + 2000);           // newer, but a DIFFERENT plugin\n      const want = path.join(base, 'claude-code-boss-abcd1234');\n      assertEq(dataDirLib.bootstrapMostRecent(), want);\n      assertEq(dataDirLib.dataDir(), want);\n      // and bootstrapping self-publishes the pick so the next env-less call is O(1)\n      assertEq(dataDirLib.readActivePointer(), want);\n    } finally { process.env.CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA = saved; }\n  });\n});\n\ntest('data-dir: falls back to the stable home path when nothing exists', () => {\n  // (d) no env, no pointer, no siblings → the bare, stable home fallback.\n  withTempHome((home) => {\n    const saved = process.env.CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA;\n    try {\n      delete process.env.CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA;\n      assertEq(dataDirLib.dataDir(),\n        path.join(home, '.claude', 'plugins', 'data', 'claude-code-boss'));\n    } finally { process.env.CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA = saved; }\n  });\n});\n\ntest('data-dir: REJECTS an unexpanded ${...} placeholder (no literal-dir split-brain)', () => {\n  // TEETH: some hook contexts don't substitute ${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA}; a naive\n  // `env || fallback` would return the literal \"${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA}\" as a dir,\n  // splitting state away from the guarded scripts. dataDir() must fall back.\n  withTempHome((home) => {\n    const saved = process.env.CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA;\n    try {\n      process.env.CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA = '${CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA}';\n      const d = dataDirLib.dataDir();\n      assert(!d.includes('${'), `must not resolve to a placeholder dir, got ${d}`);\n      assertEq(d, path.join(home, '.claude', 'plugins', 'data', 'claude-code-boss'));\n    } finally { process.env.CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA = saved; }\n  });\n});\n\ntest('data-dir: REJECTS an empty-string env (falls back)', () => {\n  withTempHome((home) => {\n    const saved = process.env.CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA;\n    try {\n      process.env.CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA = '';\n      assertEq(dataDirLib.dataDir(),\n        path.join(home, '.claude', 'plugins', 'data', 'claude-code-boss'));\n    } finally { process.env.CLAUDE_PLUGIN_DATA = saved; }\n  });\n});\n\ntest('data-dir: validEnvDir mirrors the guard (unit)', () => {\n  assertEq(dataDirLib.validEnvDir('/real/dir'), '/real/dir');\n  assertEq(dataDirLib.validEnvDir('${X}'), null);\n  assertEq(dataDirLib.validEnvDir(''), null);\n  assertEq(dataDirLib.validEnvDir(undefined), null);\n});\n\ntest('data-dir: pointer helpers round-trip; readActivePointer null for missing/corrupt/stale', () => {\n  withTempHome((home) => {\n    // globalDir() is the stable, cross-folder-invariant home; the pointer lives there.\n    assertEq(dataDirLib.globalDir(), path.join(home, '.claude', 'claude-code-boss'));\n    assertEq(path.dirname(dataDirLib.activePointerPath()), dataDirLib.globalDir());\n    // missing pointer file → null\n    assertEq(dataDirLib.readActivePointer(), null);\n    // round-trip a real, existing dir\n    const real = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'ccb-ptr-'));\n    dataDirLib.writeActivePointer(real);\n    assertEq(dataDirLib.readActivePointer(), real);\n    // stale dir (recorded but since deleted) self-heals → null\n    const gone = path.join(os.tmpdir(), `ccb-ptr-gone-${Date.now()}`);\n    dataDirLib.writeActivePointer(gone);\n    assertEq(dataDirLib.readActivePointer(), null);\n    // corrupt JSON → null (tolerated, not thrown)\n    fs.writeFileSync(dataDirLib.activePointerPath(), '{not json');\n    assertEq(dataDirLib.readActivePointer(), null);\n  });\n});\n\ntest('data-dir: writeActivePointer does NOT regress from a heavier live folder to a lighter stray (anti apparent-loss)', () => {\n  withTempHome(() => {\n    const A = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'ccb-wA-'));\n    const B = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'ccb-wB-'));\n    fs.mkdirSync(path.join(A, 'brain', 'proj'), { recursive: true });\n    fs.mkdirSync(path.join(B, 'brain', 'proj'), { recursive: true });\n    // A = the rich, live KB (big brain.db); B = a near-empty stray identity.\n    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(A, 'brain', 'proj', 'brain.db'), Buffer.alloc(50 * 1024, 1));\n    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(B, 'brain', 'proj', 'brain.db'), Buffer.alloc(1 * 1024, 1));\n    // A publishes first → pointer = A.\n    dataDirLib.writeActivePointer(A);\n    assertEq(dataDirLib.readActivePointer(), A);\n    // B (the lighter stray) tries to take the pointer → REFUSED (this is the fix:\n    // env-less brain_count/search never gets yanked onto the near-empty folder).\n    dataDirLib.writeActivePointer(B);\n    assertEq(dataDirLib.readActivePointer(), A, 'pointer must NOT
